- **scenario-planning-monte-carlo**: Generates Base / Bull / Bear scenarios + sensitivity tornado + Monte Carlo simulation on top of the tied three-statement model. Surfaces results as probability distributions ("P(runway &lt; 12 months) = 18%") rather than single-point estimates. 2026 standard: read results as odds, not anxiety. ([CraftOS-dev/craftbot-agent-bundles](https://github.com/CraftOS-dev/craftbot-agent-bundles/tree/HEAD/agents/finance-agent/skills/scenario-planning-monte-carlo) · ★10)

- **scenario-planning-trace**: Use this skill when the user invokes /scenario-planning-trace or asks for a default public reasoning trace using Scenario Planning. - **scene-scriptwriter**: 编写详细的场景剧本，定义每个场景的出场人物、对白/旁白、动作指示、镜头提示和情绪节奏。是连接角色设计与分镜生图的桥梁。 ([buda-ai/bunny-agent](https://github.com/buda-ai/bunny-agent/tree/HEAD/templates/videodrone-agent/.claude/skills/scene-scriptwriter) · ★29 · 2 copies)

- **scene-sequencing**: Structure scenes and control pacing using scene-sequel rhythm. Based on Dwight Swain's Goal-Conflict-Disaster and Reaction-Dilemma-Decision structure.
